# Skills · M · part 91

901 entries, meu-direct to mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **meu-direct**: Monta, passo a passo junto com a pessoa, o painel "Meu Direct", um ManyChat próprio e gratuito para Instagram, ligado à API oficial pelo Zernio. Quem comenta a palavra-chave recebe a DM na hora, com resposta pública no comentário, botão com link, opção "só para seguidores", resposta a story, menção e perguntas de abertura. Confere os pré-requisitos, dá ao painel as cores e o nome da marca da pessoa, conecta a chave do Zernio, cria a primeira automação e testa de verdade. Também abre o painel depois de pronto. Use quando a pessoa disser "/meu-direct", "meu manychat", "automação de comentário",… ([DivosdaIA/meu-direct](https://github.com/DivosdaIA/meu-direct/tree/HEAD/skills/meu-direct))
- **meu-negocio**: Monta, atualiza e usa a pasta que faz a IA conhecer o negócio da pessoa (o que ela vende, preços, clientes, pendências e regras do setor). Use quando ela disser que quer que a IA conheça o negócio dela, pedir para montar ou atualizar a pasta do negócio ou a pasta ai-tools, ou colar uma mensagem de cliente pedindo ajuda para responder. ([eldermoraes/ai-tools](https://github.com/eldermoraes/ai-tools/tree/HEAD/skills/meu-negocio) · ★1)
- **meu-planning**: Build a campaign plan backward from a MEU (Monthly Engaging Users) target. Use when the user has a MEU target and needs to know what campaigns to run, what mechanics to use, how to allocate budget, and what the realistic outcome is. Works for fintech apps, super apps, and mobile payment platforms in Southeast Asia. Input: MEU target, current baseline, budget, available channels, user segments, constraints. Output: gap analysis, campaign recommendations, budget allocation, confidence level, Plan B. ([thaolst/ai-growth-agents-for-marketers](https://github.com/thaolst/ai-growth-agents-for-marketers/tree/HEAD/skills/meu-planning) · ★6)
- **meu-rendimento**: Operação por IA — o rendimento do PRÓPRIO operador no app num período (pedidos, consultas, ações, cards conduzidos, envios, falhas, fluxos parados) a partir de gerar_relatorio, sem texto dos pedidos. Quem tem a Visão do Gestor vê a equipe pela /auditoria-operacao. Use quando o operador pedir "/meu-rendimento", "meu rendimento", "como eu usei a IA", "minhas métricas da semana". ([SamuelGuibor/dpvat_parana](https://github.com/SamuelGuibor/dpvat_parana/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/meu-rendimento))
- **meus-salvos**: Transforma os posts salvos do Instagram do usuário numa biblioteca de arquivos markdown que o Claude consegue ler — baixa os posts, transcreve os vídeos localmente e monta um índice. Use tanto para MONTAR a biblioteca quanto para CONSULTAR depois (planejar conteúdo, modelar anúncios, achar uma receita/treino/viagem que ele salvou, escrever no estilo de alguém que ele admira). Dispara em "meus salvos", "posts salvos", "salvos do Instagram", "biblioteca de salvos", "o que eu salvei", "baixar meus salvos", "instagram salvos". ([joaobrancoia/meus-salvos](https://github.com/joaobrancoia/meus-salvos/tree/HEAD/skills/meus-salvos) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **mev**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/mev) · ★745)
- **mev**: MEV protection against sandwich attacks and front-running ([recogardtech/AutoPilotPM](https://github.com/recogardtech/AutoPilotPM/tree/HEAD/src/skills/bundled/mev) · ★81 · 6 copies)
- **mev**: KIP-249 MEV Auction system on Kaia — slot-based backrun auction with hidden bidding. Use when developers ask about MEV on Kaia, searcher strategies, or the auction system. NOT like Ethereum's PBS/Flashbots. ([Pieverse-Eng/purrfect-skills](https://github.com/Pieverse-Eng/purrfect-skills/tree/HEAD/kaia-skills/vendor/mev))

- **mfg-machine-elements-mechanisms-and-tolerances**: Use when specifying parts and how they fit together: machine elements including bearings, fasteners, springs, gears and shafts, mechanisms and kinematics with degrees of freedom and linkages, and tolerances and geometric dimensioning and tolerancing — what a tolerance actually costs and why over-tolerancing is the expensive default. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/manufacturing-mechanical-engineering-for-software-devs/skills/mfg-machine-elements-mechanisms-and-tolerances) · ★5)
- **mfg-mechanics-stress-fatigue-and-materials**: Use for the mechanics underneath a physical design: what mechanical engineers actually do, stress, strain and deflection, failure theories and safety factors, fatigue and why most real failures are fatigue failures rather than overload, and materials selection with the property trade-offs that drive it. Includes the router for the whole manufacturing and mechanical engineering reference. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/manufacturing-mechanical-engineering-for-software-devs/skills/mfg-mechanics-stress-fatigue-and-materials) · ★5)
- **mfg-oee-analysis**: Calculate and diagnose Overall Equipment Effectiveness (OEE) by decomposing into Availability, Performance, and Quality rates. Use this skill when the user needs to measure production line efficiency, identify equipment losses, benchmark manufacturing performance, or justify capital investment — even if they say 'why is our output low', 'machine utilization report', 'production efficiency', or 'how much capacity are we losing'. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msms-data-qc-validation))
- **mgf-msms-feature-import-qiime2**: Use when starting a Qemistree/QIIME 2 metabolomics workflow with pre-processed LC-MS/MS feature data: you have an MGF file (MS1 + MS2 peaks) from feature detection and need it as a QIIME 2 artifact to run SIRIUS/ZODIAC/CSI:FingerID steps. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msms-feature-import-qiime2))
- **mgf-msp-library-format-conversion**: Use when building an MS-DIAL identification library from multiple public tandem-MS sources in mixed formats (msp and mgf) that need polarity separation and structure-field harmonization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-msp-library-format-conversion))
- **mgf-parameter-block-extraction**: Use when the input is a text-format mass spectrometry peak list file in MGF format (or a format jmzReader can export to MGF) and you need structured per-spectrum records with parameters like TITLE, PEPMASS, CHARGE, RTINSECONDS and peak arrays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-parameter-block-extraction))
- **mgf-spectra-conversion-and-database-searching**: Use when starting from vendor raw MS/MS files of immunopeptidomics (or similar enzymatic-digestion-free) experiments that must be converted to MGF and searched with multiple engines before Percolator-based rescoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-spectra-conversion-and-database-searching))
- **mgf-spectra-export**: Use when starting from Thermo RAW (or mzML/mzXML) LC-HRMS2 DDA files that must be converted into a feature quantification table plus per-feature MS2 .mgf spectra for each sample individually, before annotation or molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-export--ac63dc295294572d))
- **mgf-spectra-export**: Use when a Spectra::Spectra object has been filtered or normalized (e.g., via filterSpectriPy/normalize_intensities) and the resulting peak lists must be written out as an MGF file for storage or downstream annotation workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-export--0a5742569b420698))
- **mgf-spectra-file-handling**: Use when LC-MS/MS acquisitions (e.g. serum or food/feed samples) are exported as MGF files and the goal is direct tandem-spectrum comparison — phylogenetics, species identification, or dataset QC — before any database search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-file-handling))
- **mgf-spectra-file-parsing**: Use when an NPLinker-based integrative omics workflow needs MS/MS spectra read from a GNPS molecular networking MGF file, e.g. as the input for CANOPUS class prediction on spectra below 850 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-file-parsing))
- **mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering**: Use when assembling experimental MS/MS benchmarking sets from GNPS or third-party .mgf spectral libraries, and noisy, oversized, or adduct-mixed spectra must be normalized before annotation-tool evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering))
